# Skills · P · part 20

850 entries, parallel-dispatch to parallel-work. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **parallel-dispatch**: Use when 2+ independent tasks can run concurrently without shared state or sequential dependencies. Trigger whenever the user requests multiple things in a single message and the tasks don't depend on each other -- e.g., "run robustness checks 40 and 47", "search for papers on X and Y", "rewrite section 3 and compile the paper", "download these 3 papers and run the analysis". Also trigger when you identify parallelizable subtasks within a larger request, even if the user didn't explicitly ask for concurrency. If two things CAN run at the same time, they SHOULD. ([shusuke-ioku/r2](https://github.com/shusuke-ioku/r2/tree/HEAD/src/r2/template/.claude/skills/parallel-dispatch))
- **parallel-dispatch**: Use when facing 2+ independent tasks that can be worked on without shared state or sequential dependencies. Use when user says "並列", "並列で", "parallel", "parallel-dispatch", or whenever multiple unrelated investigations / fixes appear. ([trapple/skills](https://github.com/trapple/skills/tree/HEAD/parallel-dispatch))
- **parallel-dispatch-dag**: Constructs, validates, and traverses a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) from scope cards for safe level-based parallel dispatch. Determines execution order via topological sort. Detects cycles and invalid dependencies. ([irahardianto/rugged-gemini](https://github.com/irahardianto/rugged-gemini/tree/HEAD/.gemini/skills/parallel-dispatch-dag) · ★5)
- **parallel-dispatch-decomposition**: Decomposes broad tasks into MECE, parallelizable sub-tasks with explicit scope cards. Core skill for intra-domain parallel dispatch. Produces scope cards consumed by parallel-dispatch-dag, parallel-dispatch-ownership, and parallel-dispatch-merge skills. ([irahardianto/rugged-gemini](https://github.com/irahardianto/rugged-gemini/tree/HEAD/.gemini/skills/parallel-dispatch-decomposition) · ★5)
- **parallel-dispatch-merge**: Safe, sequential merge protocol for integrating N parallel worktree branches back into main. Defines merge ordering, quality gates between merges, conflict classification, and the updated worktree naming convention for multi-instance dispatches. ([irahardianto/rugged-gemini](https://github.com/irahardianto/rugged-gemini/tree/HEAD/.gemini/skills/parallel-dispatch-merge) · ★5)
- **parallel-dispatch-ownership**: Enforces MECE file boundaries for parallel write-agents. Validates that no two writers share file access. Manages the contracts layer (shared reads) and integration sub-task patterns. The safety invariant that prevents merge chaos. ([irahardianto/rugged-gemini](https://github.com/irahardianto/rugged-gemini/tree/HEAD/.gemini/skills/parallel-dispatch-ownership) · ★5)

- **parallel-exclude-domains-is-ignored-if-3c37907aace67356**: # Parallel source policy: exclude_domains is ignored when include_domains is set From Parallel's official Source Policy docs, the interaction rules that break filtering: - `include_domains` is an allow list. Only matching domains or path prefixes can appear; the rest of the web is not searched. Never use it as a soft preference. - `exclude_domains` is applied ONLY when `include_domains` is empty. Send both and your block list is silently ignored. - `after_date` (Search API) i Use when hitting th ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/parallel-exclude-domains-is-ignored-if-3c37907aace67356))
- **parallel-exclude-domains-is-ignored-if-ab4e47e4448dd758**: Parallel official Source Policy docs (docs.parallel.ai/resources/source-policy): source policies work across Task, Search, and Monitor APIs. include_domains is an allow list: only matching sources are returned. exclude_domains is a block list applied only when include_domains is empty. after_date (Search API) is an RFC 3339 date lower bound.
